# D2 skills-and-memory register, 2026-10-11 (lane audit2-d2-skills-and-memory, audit pass 2, FACT lane)

Read-only. Charter: audit2-charter.md (read whole). Scope: fsi-app/.claude/skills/** (8 skills, 48 files, 297 sections), CLAUDE.md at the repo root (on-disk text, current to master 0d0766347), docs/INDEX.md and the 354 docs it lists, docs not listed. The root `.claude/skills/ledger` and `.claude/commands/done.md` are D1 scope (`.claude/**`); they are read here because they define or duplicate the same behaviours (marked "adjacent").

Token meanings used in every row: `[CONFIRMED: <command> -> <output>]` = a command was run and its output is the evidence; `[CONFIRMED(path-check)]` = every repo path / registry id the document cites was resolved against the tree by script and the result is stated, the prose was NOT read whole and the row does not assert the prose is true; `[HYPOTHESIS: why]` = not executed.

## 1. Summary

Counts per CLASS: SELF-MAINTENANCE 269, NONE (record) 250, CAPABILITY 170, NONE (duplicate) 8. (Class for a document is by type: specs CAPABILITY; ADRs, runbooks, inventories, doctrine, board, skills that guard process SELF-MAINTENANCE; dated plans, audits, ops, dispatches, design are records and carry `NONE (record)`, a description, not a deletion proposal.)

### 5.3 Section rows (every heading of every SKILL.md and reference; 297 rows)

Method: each `#`..`####` heading is a section; per section the script extracted cited F-ids (checked against `node fitness/runner.mjs --list`, 44 registered), invariant ids (checked against INVARIANTS and RETIRED_INVARIANTS by prefix), rule numbers (checked against `.discipline/rules/`), and backticked repo paths (checked against `git ls-files` by prefix list). STALE = at least one of those fails, or a manual contradiction (note in the TOKEN column). The five "Reference index" rows list sibling files relative to the skill directory and were excluded from the dead-path test. "no registry id or F-id cited" means the property the section governs could not be linked to a registry row by id; it does not prove no row exists (REG-1 rows are keyed by invariant id, and a section may be governed without citing it).
